# Supplied-book source inventory

This crosswalk owns source section → existing record, uncovered work, exclusion or non-content disposition, and planned author/audit owner. It classifies all 2,453 PDF pages. It is not a passage-fidelity audit, a specialist review, or a certification. The planned machine projection and decision rules are owned by the [knowledge-model audit contract](../../design-docs/knowledge-model.md#approved-versioned-audit-contract); this crosswalk remains the source-unit authority.

Edition identity, fingerprints, bibliography, rights, and discrepancy resolutions remain in [Book sources](../../references/book-sources.md) and [`sources.json`](../../../packages/knowledge/data/sources.json). Corpus claims and release requirements remain in [Knowledge quality](../../product-specs/knowledge-quality.md). Stable record and citation contracts remain in [Knowledge model](../../design-docs/knowledge-model.md). The roadmap routes all planned authoring and audit features in [Knowledge roadmap](../../../features/knowledge-roadmap.md).

## Reading the dispositions

| Label                                 | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Selected coverage; remainder open** | Released records or citations support selected claims only. They do not cover the whole source unit or its actual layers.                                                     |
| **Uncovered**                         | The source material is present, but no matching authored record/citation was found in the current manifest crosswalk. It is not excluded.                                     |
| **Source-reported omission**          | The supplied edition explicitly marks material missing, reduced, untranslated, or represented elsewhere. Inventory the extant statement/text; do not reconstruct absent text. |
| **Unlocated; unresolved**             | A source reference or attribution target was not found. Keep it visible; do not claim source-wide absence.                                                                    |
| **Non-content disposition**           | A cover, divider, blank, contents page, plate, or colophon has an explicit navigation/editorial disposition, not a doctrinal claim.                                           |
| **Pending owner review**              | The source unit remains included in the work queue. Finer passage, image, attribution, or claim decisions belong to its named feature.                                        |

PDF pages are one-based and include covers. Printed labels are a separate evidence field. An extraction match is not an image-verified printed label. A blank heading search is not evidence of source absence. The exclusive ranges below partition pages; nested figures, lines, headings, and shared-page locators are child units and never change those ranges.

## Edition page partitions

### BPCT — 467 pages

These stable IDs map to exclusive, contiguous PDF intervals. `Niên Thời` is unnumbered between chapters 7 and 9. Chapter 20 and its supplement are separate units. Fresh page-image checks of BPCT PDFs 42, 279, 413 and 467 confirm that the first three show only printed folios 35, 241 and 366, respectively, and PDF 467 is blank. Keep these dispositions separate from passage omission.

## Quẻ register and source-unit expectations

Each row gives the observed source interval for one quẻ in all three classical editions. The stable child IDs are `pbc-hexagram-NN`, `ntt-hexagram-NN`, and `nhl-hexagram-NN`, using the row's two-digit number. Quẻ 01–60 map to authored released JSON `hexagram-01.json` through `hexagram-60.json` and selected citations; this does not mean that all three books, every layer, or every passage is covered. Quẻ 61–64 have no authored released JSON in the current manifest and are **uncovered**, not excluded.

For every quẻ, derive the source-unit expectation before consulting authored counts: (1) title, number, name and overview/figure; (2) classical text and Thoán text/commentary; (3) Tượng material, including distinct Đại/Tiểu Tượng where present; (4) positions 1–6, bottom to top, each with source line text and each actually present commentary/translator layer; (5) notes, named quotations, diagrams and cross-references where present. Presence or absence of any layer is a per-source finding, not assumed for all editions. Càn/Khôn special passages are independent children, not line 7. Do not attempt the deferred 1,152 book-position audit cells in this inventory.

### Part I, chapter 6: printed labels 1–69

## Fresh source inspection and limits

**Comparison record:** Prepared by GPT-6 Luna (AI assistant) on 2026-10-03. Scope was the four supplied PDF editions, current manifest-listed record/citation routes, edition fingerprints, and the bounded external source-inspection reports/maps. Method: reconcile source-reported exclusive page partitions with extracted heading/page text and image-check only disputed or disposition-critical BPCT pages; cross-reference selected existing citations without treating them as whole-section coverage. Named editor/translator/classical layers were kept distinct where the source or evidence identifies them. This is a source-inventory comparison, not independent specialist approval or passage-fidelity certification.

For BPCT, the actual command used PyMuPDF 1.28.0 (`python3 - <<'PY'`, importing `fitz` and reading `Page.get_text()`): it read text at the first PDF page of each of the 44 listed exclusive top-level units, scanned board pages 49–66, and checked the named transition/ending pages. Output confirmed the section-title contexts on the 44 interval starts where extractable; the chapter-4 board heading sequence; the Part II transition at PDF 365, Hà Tri at PDF 414, casting supplement at PDF 429, and Part III at PDF 458. The corresponding source reports/maps supply page-level boundary details where a start page begins with continuation material or extracted ordering is imperfect. This extraction supports navigation only. Direct image artifacts freshly opened in this session were BPCT PDFs 42, 57, 279, 413, 432, 460, 464, 467 and all pages 77–100. The sparse pages show only folios 35/241/366 or are blank; PDF 432 directly shows II ending and IV beginning with no III heading; PDFs 460 and 464 show criticism headings V and XIII. PDF 57 confirms Chấn board 6; 77–100 confirms chapter-6 labels 1–69, including 11 and 60. No semantic review is claimed.

All four supplied PDFs were opened with PyMuPDF 1.28.0 for page counts and extraction. Source inspection evidence varies by edition; only the pages and contact sheets explicitly recorded below are counted as visually reviewed. The exact source files and expected SHA-256/page counts are owned by [`sources.json`](../../../packages/knowledge/data/sources.json); inventory inspection does not change those fingerprints.

Visual evidence recorded by the source-inspection lanes is limited to artifacts explicitly reported as opened and reviewed; generating a render or contact sheet alone does not count as visual review:

- **BPCT:** Fresh individual images directly reviewed for this inventory were PDFs 42, 57, 279, 413, 432, 460, 464, 467 and every page PDF 77–100. Earlier generated boundary/board/question/supplement/criticism sheets have no established review provenance here and are not counted as inspected.
- **PBC:** The source report records visual review of four contact sheets covering all 64 quẻ heading/diagram pages, plus selected rendered pages for front matter, figures, special passages, Hệ Từ and end matter. It also records full-size checks of line-heading pages 108, 202, 243, 272, 373, 420, 474, 519, 527, 552 and 561. These checks do not establish the unverified per-quẻ layer candidates as present or absent.
- **NTT:** The bounded unit map records selective direct image checks at PDFs 20–22, 27–47, 50, 63–65, 77–80 and 126, with named figure and layer anchors detailed above. Generated contact sheets and other renders are not treated as reviewed beyond those recorded checks.
- **NHL:** The source report records direct individual-page review at PDFs 1, 4, 15–16, 22, 28, 53, 64, 72–73, 139–140, 246, 268–269, 313 and 332–333, and opening contact sheet `sheet-01` for PDFs 1–4. Other generated pages and contact sheets are not asserted as reviewed.

Unverified candidates remain navigation leads, not absence findings. Each row below maps one board to its own V2 figure and claims in batch eighteen.
Six labels preserve source order lục→sơ; polarities were read from images independently,
not generated from the core. Exact printed annotations are source observations, not corrected
calculation facts. Parenthetical Phục labels identify the flying host at that line; no separate
Phi column is printed. Absence means no label on this inspected figure, not absence in all editions.
Commentary is separately attributed to the compilation through Vĩnh Cao. Notes are not absorbed.

### Feat-051 final layer and release reconciliation

All 196 assigned parent/child obligations map to the fifteen new V2 records; no existing stable
unit or exclusion is removed. The registry expands from 518 to 625 groups, retaining all 17
previous exclusions and all unrelated ownership, locators, mappings and discovery states.
It also separates observed Vĩnh Cao and Trương Cảnh Tùng prefaces from uncredited supplements;
attached-note children use their translator-note scope rather than assuming every vocabulary layer.
These explicit edition-scoped rows remain unresolved for later audit; no independent layer decision
is recorded. Source-review and certification work remains open (generated gates remain closed).

Chapter-one verse and meaning claims explicitly distinguish X, XIV, XIX, XXII–XXIV; XIV has
no independently printed meaning paragraph beyond the instruction and placement example.
All twelve printed Hỏa-stage positions and the exact partial Thủy/Mộc/Kim/Thổ rosters are accounted
for without filling lược marks. Existing typed Na Jia, palace, element, relative and coin tables stay
unchanged. New rosters not fitting those bounded table kinds use original prose facts; the two-column
Lục thú example uses a bounded, evidence-linked placement figure. No generic table DSL or new
calculation authority is introduced. The full per-claim citation IDs resolve through
`citations/batch-eighteen-advanced.json`; row parent bounds are not evidence for an unrelated layer.

Precise exclusions/non-authority dispositions are local to each inspected claim: uncredited
identity is not inferred; source-reported/observed missing entries (Tân, rendering lines, stage
lược marks and absent board labels) stay missing; malformed/contradictory annotations remain
reported source readings rather than repaired calculation facts. Historical social, gender, health,
death, ritual and efficacy assertions are included only as attributed ideas, never modern facts or
instructions. The lone non-content child `bpct-ch02-folio-42` accounts for its printed 35 and spacing.
This completes assigned authoring comparison, not feat-084 audit or global remainder reconciliation.

For boards with the entire prose beginning on the next page, commentary citations now start
on that observed prose page (07:51, 10:52, 14:53, 21:55, 28:57, 35:59, 43:61, 47:62,
51:63, 55:64, 59:65, 63:66), not the heading/figure page. Registry board bounds remain
the complete unit bounds spanning heading, figure and commentary; figure citations stay separate.

## Feat-052 application passage dispositions

Source comparison uses the catalog BPCT fingerprint (467 pages). Complete extracted passages at
PDF 101–165 and neighboring 100/166 were read. Contact sheets covering 100–163 and individual
images 164–166 were inspected, with focused individual images 137 and 143. This is not a claim
of individual full-size image review of every page. No table or diagram occurs in these six units.
Printed folios restart/repeat: 101–110 = 85–94; 111–118 = 94–101; 119–141 = 101–123;
142–148 = 118–124; 149–155 = 124–130; 156–165 = 130–139.

The rows below are stable child obligations from source inspection before authoring. V/R/C means
original plus Hán-Việt reading / independently printed Vietnamese meaning / author commentary;
these are source layers, not a reconstructed edition. A dash denotes an inspected absent block,
not an omission to be supplied. Shared-page and continued blocks retain both endpoints.
Notes have their own attribution and owner, regardless of footer placement. The chapter-9 inserted
essay has thirty original and thirty Vietnamese items plus a closing qualification. Its website
credit does not identify an individual author or translator; it is not verse 81. Chapter 10 has
two printed 5 labels, kept as 05a/05b without renumbering later labels. PDF155 continues the
chapter-11 closing verse. PDF165 is folio-only, not a lost closing fragment; the complete chapter-12
closing is on164. Chapter12's exclusive parent 156–165 stays unchanged.

All rows route authoring to feat-052 and later audit to feat-086. Discovery, layer audit, separate
verification and certification remain unresolved; a source-compared release is not an audit decision.
The named historical examples and outcome assertions are source testimony, never efficacy evidence.
No copied verse, translation, source image, calculation repair, calendar, automatic classifier,
medical/mortality/financial advice, coercive gender/class duty, ritual requirement or certification
is released. Contradictions and attribution limitations are recorded in [Book sources](../../references/book-sources.md).

## Feat-053 application passage dispositions

The exact catalog BPCT SHA-256 `713f6f5b170a8e9e2dc39c137a2d18488bb5f185cdac1ade621953b5fa3f897a`
and 467 pages were verified. Complete extracted passages on PDF166–230 were read;
contact sheets covering165–231 were inspected. Individual images1–3,190,219,222 were
inspected for credits, boundaries and extraction-sensitive text. This does not claim individual
full-size inspection of every page. No table or diagram occurs in these seven units.
Printed folios repeat at chapter joins:166–175=139–148;176–183=148–155;
184–190=155–161;191–199=161–169;200–211=169–180;212–222=180–190;
223–230=190–197. Neighboring165 is folio-only; chapter20 begins231 and is not authored here.

The actual source corrects two planning assumptions, with coordinator approval after individual
image inspection: PDF190 is folio161 only (chapter15 closing wholly on189); PDF222 is folio190
only (chapter18 item42 wholly on221). No continuation or missing prose is invented. Parent
intervals remain184–190/212–222. The observed opening of chapter14 says23; do not infer a
missing22. Chapter15 skips printed16, chapter17 skips31, and chapter18 skips41. These are
numbering gaps, not evidence of absent passages to reconstruct. Chapter14 item7 has a reading
and commentary but no independently printed Vietnamese meaning. Extraction gaps in Han glyphs
are not assertions of missing text in the page image.

Stable child obligations below were mapped before authoring. V/R/C denotes original plus
Hán-Việt reading / independently printed Vietnamese meaning / commentary. A dash means the
block is not independently printed. Footnotes are separate translator layers; folio-only children
are non-content. Parent and child discovery and layer resolution remain unresolved for feat-087;
no audit, separate verification or certification decision is created. General front credits name
Lưu Bá Ôn for phú, Vương Hồng Tự for compilation/commentary and Vĩnh Cao for translation/notes,
not certain individual authorship of each verse. No previous direct citations were found on these
assigned pages; prior released claims and citations remain unchanged.

Precise non-authority dispositions apply to each historical claim: no verified outcomes/efficacy,
calendar, automatic interpretation, suspect profiling, relationship duties/coercion, childbirth or
medical/safety/financial guidance. Original summaries may describe the historical belief without
endorsing it. Source disagreements and qualifications stay separate; no copied source prose/image
or PDF runtime path is published. Publication is bounded source comparison, not global completion.
